* Encrypted restore exports into one exact new staging path, never an existing generation. Generic BackupDatabase
  cannot convert plaintext/cipher formats. Final profile authorization/key/crash-safe commit remains separate.

## 119. Complete typed staging verification (D-187)

* Before generation acceptance compare logical schema/header, all typed rows/duplicate multiplicity
  and exact text/BLOB bytes; verify relational and cipher integrity. Do not log financial values.
* Use direct native statement spans for expression BLOB columns; the maintained driver metadata
  lookup caused an observed native crash. Preserve its negative evidence and real platform checks.
* Verification is read-only and never commits a pointer or authorizes recovery/profile/key decisions.

## 120. Non-secret generation protocol checkpoints (D-188)

* Bind transitions to one profile and distinct explicit generations. Advance only in order; original
  stays selected until selector commit. Never infer plaintext fallback from a missing candidate key.
* The immutable model does not certify durable effects. Actual key persistence, staged verification,
  selector storage/reopen, exact cleanup and restart fault injection remain coordinator obligations.

## 121. Resumable migration coordinator (D-189)

* Coordinate only an exclusively captured profile writer session. Read actual stored progress,
  reject regression/foreign identities, and revalidate context/cancellation after native awaits.
* Compare-and-commit then read back every checkpoint before the next effect. Selected restart
  reopens the candidate with its key before exact cleanup; never fall back to plaintext.
* Native effects must be idempotent after interruptions. Faultable port tests prove coordination,
  not actual key/filesystem/power-loss durability; require real adapters and restart proof before activation.

## 122. Non-root Native CI bind ownership (D-190)

* Linux native containers use the numeric non-root host UID/GID and one fresh owned bind-mounted
  scratch directory at the recipe's original path. Keep source/recipe mounts read-only and network disabled.
* Test real Linux permission refusal/matched ownership plus actual Android/upstream mount arguments.
  Do not fix ownership by making output world-writable or running native compilation as root.
* Preserve and surface failed native build logs. Workspace proof is separate from full hosted native
  build/security suite results; do not repeat unchanged application tests/UI/APKs for a tooling-only fix.

## 123. Native work parent traversal (D-191)

* Matching bind UID is insufficient if its parent is private to the image user. The immutable
  build home permits traversal (0755), not foreign writes; actual work/output remain separately owned.
* Exercise a foreign non-root UID through the actual work mount even on Docker Desktop. Preserve
  the failing old-image evidence and require actual hosted native completion after corrections.

## 124. Approved key recovery and permanent OS-backup policy (D-192)

* OD-04 is settled: automatic local profile key, optional separate recovery key and Zanance backup
  recovery. Portable backup passwords remain optional. Do not ask this product choice again.
* OD-10 is settled: Android/iOS databases and sensitive files are excluded from OS backup/transfer
  with completed encryption integration; new devices restore Zanance backups with fresh local keys.
  No interim policy. Native storage/migration/recovery/platform acceptance still requires implementation.
* Bind platform envelopes to profile, installation, generation and format. Windows uses CurrentUser
  DPAPI, not LocalMachine; do not claim hardware/device-only protection or same-user malware resistance.

## 125. Android installation wrapping key (D-193)

* Use application-scoped AndroidKeyStore AES-256/GCM with native randomized nonces and bound
  profile/installation/generation context. Prepare explicitly; unwrap never creates/replaces a key.
* Reject malformed/tampered envelopes and exportable wrapping keys. Automatic access is independent
  of the app PIN; run native effects off the UI thread under the captured access/migration gate.
* Report actual OS security level without inferring hardware protection. Independent Release process
  proof is separate from durable storage/recovery/migration and main-app encryption acceptance.

## 126. Immutable Android envelope files (D-194)

* Store only authenticated opaque envelopes in an existing trusted app-private directory under the
  exclusive captured access/profile gate. Derive final names only from bound profile/generation IDs.
* Create the final envelope exclusively, flush it and fsync its directory. Android app-private link
  publication was refused with EACCES. Torn files fail closed and are never replaced with a new key.
* Reads use native no-follow/nonblocking open and regular-file validation with bounded fixed lengths.
  Never create missing keys or choose orphan staging files. Process restart is not power-loss acceptance.

## 127. Transactional non-secret generation journal (D-195)

* Keep the migration selector in an explicitly created, versioned private SQLite protocol file;
  only bound profile/generation IDs and ordered phase belong there, never keys or financial data.
* Use rollback DELETE and synchronous EXTRA, validate captured schema/context before changing an
  existing file's mode, and compare-and-commit the next phase under an immediate transaction.
* Missing/unknown/corrupt journals never become Original automatically. Caller retains its exclusive
  access/profile lease; no file store alone authorizes opening/cleanup or a plaintext fallback.
* Verify actual native hot-journal and committed-selection process kills separately from full
  multi-resource migration, disk-pressure/hardware-power-loss and physical-platform acceptance.

## 128. Complete captured native migration effects (D-196)

* Use original coordinator/session with actual protected keys, coherent bounded memory snapshots,
  complete typed verification, encrypted candidate and encrypted rollback before selection.
* Recheck lease/identity/stored phase at native effects. Recreate only the bound unselected candidate
  with its existing key; missing selected keys never prepare replacements or choose plaintext.
* Source opens ReadWrite without Create because SQLCipher ATTACH inherits access flags for writable
  memory export/hot-journal recovery. Main is read in a snapshot transaction; no ledger writes.
* Keyed reopen and encrypted rollback verification precede exact original/sidecar cleanup. Synchronize
  closed encrypted files and directory through actual platform effects, not a managed rename claim.
* Independent original-model Android process interruption evidence does not activate main profiles,
  final backup/recovery UI, OS exclusion or certify power loss/other platforms.

## 129. Atomic protected context publication (D-197)

* Publish verified encrypted path and captured connection creator together; never cache key-bearing
  EF options by path. Key owners validate authorization and own mutable credential lifetime.
* Protected creators return closed unpooled keyed ReadWrite connections to the exact existing file,
  never Create mode. Verify ciphertext and reviewed provider before EF work; no plaintext fallback.
* Retire protected publications even at the same path; already-open EF contexts reject commands.
  This does not replace a global exclusive reader/writer lease or guard arbitrary direct native SQL.
* The legacy online-copy backup source remains permanently plaintext-only and refuses keyed contexts
  before stream/temp work. Final generation-aware portable backup/restore is a separate composition.

## 130. Protected portable backup export (D-198)

* Keyed factory sources export a coherent bounded portable image through SQLCipher memory only;
  no plaintext temporary file or online cipher/plain page copy. Portable password choice stays optional.
* Validate captured connection publication and cached commercial access before/after native effects
  and every output chunk. Keep chunk size independent from ArrayPool capacity; partial output is
  caller-owned on failure and must never be published as a successful backup.
* Weak connection/access association stores no key material. Existing-only encrypted generation
  restore/selection still requires its own receiver; legacy plaintext restore refuses keyed contexts.

## 131. Encrypted restored staging (D-199)

* Read portable images into bounded owned memory; refuse existing candidate/sidecars before input
  consumption and prepare a fresh captured device binding only after input integrity/access checks.
* Verify complete imported schema/header/typed rows before actual application migrations, reopen
  upgraded candidate for cipher/relational integrity and synchronize it. No plaintext temporary file.
* Candidate preparation alone never selects live data, deletes originals or certifies safety rollback;
  generation-aware selection/package transaction and restart acceptance remain separate obligations.

## 132. Restored selection and encrypted rollback (D-200)

* Restored candidate selection reads its existing bound key; it never generates a replacement.
  Preserve current data in fully verified/synchronized encrypted rollback before journal selection.
* Use configured connection identity, not SQLite open-time filename aliases. Compare/read back every
  ordered checkpoint; Selected restart opens candidate/rollback without old connection or plaintext fallback.
* Retain original/rollback and Selected state until complete package/lifecycle transaction finalizes;
  this primitive does not restore preferences, publish contexts, prune keys or certify full recovery.
* Observation timeout is not process failure. Resume the same live barrier; fix diagnosed Mono
  initialization before restarting only the stuck resume phase, never re-create its existing candidate.

## 133. Whole-package restore dispatch (D-201)

* An explicitly registered package receiver exclusively owns every validated entry under the backup gate.
  Receiver failure never falls back to per-source writes. Unknown entries stay available for receiver policy;
  absent entries do not imply deletion. No receiver retains borrowed portable buffers after completion.
* The service gate is not a global application lease. Receiver registration alone provides no durable
  package transaction; native staging, rollback, journal reconciliation and publication remain required.

## 134. Protected package preference replay (D-202)

* Stage the database and generation-bound encrypted original/restored portable-preference capsule
  before selection. Reject unsupported sources before effects; absent portable values preserve current choices.
* Resume selected packages without old connection or original portable input. Replay the exact allowlist
  before protected publication under a covered exclusive application lease. Retain capsule/database rollback/key.
* Platform preference writes alone are not durable completion. Startup must replay the capsule before unlock;
  unfinished pre-capsule staging fails closed for reconciliation, not guessed overwrite or regenerated keys.

## 135. Factory lifetime maintenance exclusion (D-203)

* Every original local-factory context owns one access lease until native/context disposal finishes.
  Pending exclusive work stops reader admission, drains existing contexts and supports cancellation.
* Maintenance contexts require the exact live owner from that location. Early owner disposal retires
  commands/publication but cannot release other work until its owned contexts close. No reader bypass.
* Publish with explicit ownership while maintenance is active. Covered lifecycle must coordinate
  background work and native effects outside the factory; the gate does not encrypt data by itself.

## 136. Original profile switch maintenance ownership (D-204)

* Take the location's maintenance owner inside automatic-posting exclusion before moving a profile.
  Destination migrations and settings reads use the same owned factory, never await ordinary admission.
* Keep the owner through authentication/catalogue commit or restoration of the previous path.
  Queued work captures its endpoint after admission; refusal never opens the temporary destination.
* Owned settings share the original settings initialization algorithm and semaphore. No guessed accounts,
  ledger posting, cipher fallback or ownership bypass is added; protected catalogue adoption remains separate.

## 137. Inactive profile deletion maintenance (D-205)

* The real profile form awaits context drain before deleting an inactive profile or its local backups.
  Recheck the captured catalogue/policy/current profile after waiting and before any mutation.
* Cancellation before deletion and stale reviews preserve original files/catalogue. Legacy synchronous
  calls acquire only immediately available ownership; never block the UI or bypass pending writers.
* Current/main deletion remains forbidden. Existing file-error cleanup policy is unchanged; final
  protected generation/key/rollback retention and deletion lifecycle are separate outstanding work.

## 138. Durable local key-binding identities (D-206)

* Private versioned identity bootstrap records one installation and nonempty key-binding profile ids,
  including the legacy empty-id main profile. Initial original/candidate generations are captured once.
* Read never creates or regenerates missing/corrupt/foreign/incorrectly typed identities. Explicit
  profile registration preserves existing committed bindings; no names, keys or paid facts are stored.
* These are initial migration identities, not a live selector. Actual journal/key/file verification
  and final catalogue/startup integration are still required; no portable or main activation follows.

## 139. Initial protected generation composition (D-207)

* Reopen actual stable identity and existing journal under the location's maintenance owner; do not
  fabricate captured ids or initialize missing selected state on read. Explicit first-journal creation
  requires the known plaintext original and no candidate/rollback.
* Join original migration/native key/durability effects, then verify the exact retained key read before
  atomic protected factory publication. Retain the key owner while active; drain contexts before disposal.
* Disposed key owners refuse further construction; no original/plaintext fallback. This initial
  composition is not a selector for later restored generations or main startup/provider activation.

## 140. Durable restore generation references (D-208)

* Reserve captured parent/target references before protected package input/key/staging effects. Keep
  ordered ancestry and initial installation/profile binding; unresolved intent cannot be replaced.
* Reopen and require the latest reference on receiver restart. Mirror selection only after actual
  journal/key/file verification and preference replay; Selected is not package/prefs durability.
* Never fall back to the initial generation after missing/corrupt reference state. Retain all prior
  references, key/capsule/rollback ownership; live startup resolution and pruning remain separate work.

## 141. One-time durable restore preference replay (D-209)

* A pending encrypted capsule replays settings, then requires an actual platform persistence callback
  before committing/readback/synchronizing Applied. Memory readback or a completed placeholder is not durability.
* Applied capsules preserve later user edits and skip replay/persistence on subsequent startup.
  Failed persistence leaves replay/reference publication pending; retained original values stay unchanged.
* Protocol v2 is explicit; old independent v1 capsules fail closed rather than silently upgrade or reset.
  Main platform persistence adapters and final startup/provider activation remain unqualified/open.

### D-210 — Persist original Android MAUI preferences before recovery completion

The Android host now registers ISettingsPersistence against the exact Preferences.Default backend
used by MAUI 10.0.110. Native PreferenceManager selects the same default SharedPreferences; a custom
IPreferences backend is refused rather than writing a guessed file. Persistence runs off the UI thread.
A fresh device-local operational marker forces a real snapshot write, rather than an empty editor
that may skip writing. Commit failure or cancellation throws; the protected receiver must not mark
its capsule Applied or publish until this completes under exclusive preference-writer ownership.
The marker is neither a secret nor a portable preference. Windows/iOS persistence, live generation
resolution, final provider/startup/recovery and sensitive-file OS exclusion remain open. The main
application database remains plaintext; registration alone does not activate encryption.

Focused acceptance compiles the original MauiSettingsStore and native persistence adapter with
actual MAUI Essentials 10.0.110 in the independent Android package. It writes only fictitious values,
checks cancellation, terminates the process, verifies a retained value and removed key in a cold
process, then cleans its own values. This is Android x64 API36/4 KiB evidence, not phone, iOS,
hardware-key, whole-application encryption or product-release acceptance.
### D-211 — Retain the restored generation through cold startup composition

SqlCipherProfileGeneration.OpenRestoredAsync resumes the actual latest package under the current
location's exclusive maintenance owner. It requires the receiver's exact private restore-history
store and installation/profile key namespace before native effects. Pending preference replay and
actual candidate/rollback/journal verification finish before retaining the exact existing key.
Readback requires the latest selected target; missing or mismatched history fails closed. No original
or initial-generation fallback, key preparation, directory guessing or early publication is added.
The retained key owner verifies the provider/integrity again and publishes through the same original
protected factory lifetime used by initial migration. Its disposal still refuses new connections.

The receiver has an internal no-publication resume path for this composition. A stale publication
callback from another process is never invoked; the current host publishes only after obtaining the
retained key owner. The public restore entry retains its existing behavior. Automatic all-profile
startup routing, main provider adoption, early staging reconciliation, optional recovery and retained
file/key lifecycle remain open. Main application storage remains plaintext. This change is not a
claim of complete encryption, device/iOS/OAuth or release acceptance.
### D-212 — Resolve the current protected generation from stable ancestry

OpenCurrentAsync reads the actual existing installation/profile catalogue and requires history's
complete initial key binding. Missing/corrupt/foreign history never implies initial selection and
cannot manufacture migration state. Row zero routes through actual initial migration/reopen;
any recorded restore intent routes through the captured latest receiver and retained restored owner.
Receiver target/parent/private directory and exact history are validated, including unresolved intents.
No enumeration, latest-file guess, key replacement or initial/plaintext fallback is introduced.

The host explicitly initializes history only after verified initial migration; interrupted bootstrap
still fails closed for reconciliation. This automatic composition is not yet registered in the main
startup/provider/profile lifecycle. Native producer acceptance, all-profile live adoption, recovery
and retained-file/key policy, early staging reconciliation and final sensitive OS exclusion remain.
No additional owner decision or commercial activation follows; main storage remains plaintext.
### D-213 — Preserve protected publication when a profile switch is refused

The original profile switch captures its complete publication inside the actual maintenance owner,
then asks the native runtime to open/migrate the destination. On authentication refusal, retired
catalogue or failed opening, rollback restores the original path and bound connection creator.
A path-only MoveTo cannot restore encrypted storage and is no longer used for this rollback.
Rollback is one-use, requires its exact live owner, never revives retired protected context identity,
and refuses a plaintext snapshot after protection is required. The host retains underlying key
owners until the transition finishes; no credentials are exposed by the publication token.

IProfileRuntime.OpenDatabaseAsync is the explicit native destination effect. The existing default
opens/migrates plaintext storage; a protected location rejects it. Final protected hosts supply
verified keyed publication, never strip protection or recreate missing plaintext. The actual MAUI
host's protected generation resolver/lifetime registration and final startup/all-profile/recovery/
retention/provider/OS-policy adoption still remain open. Main database encryption is not activated.

Concurrent initial Android/test builds hit CS2012/MSB3248 locked shared DLLs; those negative logs
are retained. Successful Windows and independent Release builds were not repeated. Only failed
Android and full test executions were retried once, sequentially after competing builds completed.
### D-214 — Own active and tentative profile keys through the real transition

SqlCipherProfileSession retains the active verified generation while a destination is tentative.
The actual ProfileService invokes finalization after committing its reviewed catalogue or restoring
its complete previous publication. The MAUI runtime forwards opening/finalization to the registered
protected session; existing plaintext hosts retain their original path and never bypass protection.
Accepted switching clears the previous mutable key owner; refused switching clears only the
candidate after the original publication is verified. No envelope, database or rollback is deleted.

Start, switch, acceptance, refusal and close require the actual live maintenance owner. Key retirement
also requires all owned contexts to be disposed. Concurrent resolution is serialized and synchronous

### D-221 — Review Windows upstream atomic-width warnings

The two C4133 diagnostics are in unchanged OpenSSL tsan_add calls for 32-bit new_nid and
global_seed. The MSVC macro type-checks both arms of a sizeof-based conditional; its unselected
64-bit arm causes the pointer diagnostic. Exact original obj_dat.obj disassembly uses lock xadd
dword ptr at new_nid. Exact property.obj uses lock add dword ptr at global_seed because its fetch
result is unused. Both original operations are 32-bit; no upstream source patch or warning suppression
was added. This is compiler/source evidence, not a claim that incompatible pointer warnings are
universally harmless. See the official [intrinsic signatures](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/cpp/intrinsics/interlockedexchangeadd-intrinsic-functions?view=msvc-170).

Test-WindowsAtomicWidth.ps1 verifies the exact header against the pinned official Git object, the
unchanged candidate DLL hash and pinned MSVC/SDK. A compiled upstream-macro probe exercises -1 + 1
with an adjacent sentinel (an incorrect 64-bit addition would carry into it), 64-bit values above
2^32 and 800,000 atomic additions across four threads. Probe disassembly separately verifies DWORD
and QWORD operations. The first verifier expected xadd for the unused global_seed result and failed;
inspection showed the valid lock add optimization. Corrected only that matching rule, then the related
probe passed. No unrelated native suite, main build/tests/UI or APK was repeated.

Static range review also bounds two C4334 diagnostics: the QUIC first-byte shift is 0..3 and its
result 1..8 fits 32 bits before widening. The two C4319 diagnostics complement a shifted unsigned
32-bit bit value, then intersect it with a final bit-0 mask; upper extension cannot affect that
condition. These are expression-level reviews, not independent acceptance of BN/QUIC algorithms.
The remaining 743 C4244/C4267 narrowing diagnostics still need review; the Windows native candidate
is not yet adopted or declared warning-free. Main encryption and the full recovery/platform scope
remain unfinished. Main tests (3,343) and signed APK are unchanged from D-220.

Full 749-occurrence inventory: artifacts/native-sqlcipher/d221-warning-inventory.json.

Require current target device/identity/root/membership/cursor/head/deadline and accepted read authority, no own rows,

A retained original candidate resumes without replacing keys; a clean exact already-applied horizon creates no new
empty financial round. Retained former-owner Viewer/Editor roles never receive the new private ownership root.

### Household permanent privacy intent (D-294 in progress)

Owner deletion retains its exact literal declaration in an existing protected local slot before HTTP. Pending or accepted intent blocks ordinary and incoming financial writers; uncertain transport errors never release it. Only the actual service's locked proof of unaccepted expiry permits explicit local finish. Preserve local money, keys, user pause and unrelated operations. Accepted access revocation is permanent, while current physical retirement may need replay after an old database restore.

The service publishes immutable minimal deletion intent outside its database snapshot before effects, checks it on every room authorization and replays exact-room cleanup. Keep its latest private marker set with database restoration; an old snapshot alone is not current privacy authority. Actor/time/unsigned activity are service-reported, and public signatures must match independently verified original membership/publication history. This is not identity erasure, local-copy recall or power-loss acceptance. Main mobile, lifecycle composition and whole D-294 remain open.
Household expiry does not remove owner privacy rights. Deletion review uses only actual owner/device-scoped opaque checkpoints and an existing independently trusted private root; no financial refresh, epoch grant or new root adoption is permitted merely to delete. Same-key session reattachment may support status/privacy metadata during retention, while every financial/key resource and writer remains separately refused. Recheck the exact pre-confirmation checkpoint before retaining original consent. Historical root anchoring for this purpose must never become roster/epoch/financial authority. Local-household opt-in is a nonsecret technical setting that must survive private backup/restart/restore and remain impossible to activate on public/production hosting.

manifest on an actual identity mismatch, never reset another installation or bypass the pre-startup guard.
The household Android identity override is Debug-only. Keep real native confirmations, protected screen flags,
original fixture keys/data and completed stages; public diagnostic observations are not Release/phone acceptance.

Household client proposal deadlines reserve one minute below the actual service maximum to tolerate ordinary
positive clock skew; never loosen the service ceiling, renew existing literal consent or change a device clock.
An explicit RemoteRefused observation overrides a last positive grant snapshot for sync/audit UI and commands.
Lifecycle metadata never renews that financial authority. Hide obsolete future automatic-retention deadlines
after permanent deletion, while retaining the original receipt and downloaded local data. The independent main
Windows/Android Debug privacy lifecycle is verified; broader household and final Release acceptance remain open.

Warm household HTTP403/404/410 failures must publish any already recorded local RemoteRefused barrier before

for authority or discard original finance/keys/pending ciphertext. A failed local refusal read closes the old
presentation hint; retired profile state cannot publish onto another context. Last verified ownership is separate
from current device write authority and existing owner-only privacy metadata rights.

Protected portable exports omit trusted device-only household/sync rows before native allocation; do not first

original source history and existing output bounds. The scoped standard native authorizer applies only to exact

before subsequent work and on failure; external packages never select omitted tables. Enforce destination page
bounds before growth, retain native failure codes/evidence, and never fork the cipher or create plaintext disk
snapshots to compact history.

Native Windows dialog sizing keeps original controls/results/commands and grows button minimum targets to44 units only while that interaction is outstanding. Always detach its popup observer. Native prompts must inherit the actual page language direction; a ContentDialog footer resource alone does not cover MAUI Flyouts/subclass styles. Preserve genuine native Cancel/confirmation and own-window-only review; layout never manufactures protocol consent or financial writes.

independent package, exact Main/OS state and phone turn, and never count one clean diagnostic cohort
as proof that a random native fault is fixed.

## D-379 withdrawn constructor tag-list candidate

Do not repeat constructor-owned empty-tag reuse without new evidence. Its complete current x64 Home
cohort preserves behavior/data but is descriptively slower in five of six corresponding samples per

order. Source allocation reasoning alone is not a whole-page benefit; retain the rejected source/
package/receipts and original data instead of promoting it, selecting fast cases or extending an
already rejected trial to the phone.


## D-380 bounded physical disclosure reviews

Before non-editor phone input, require the original IME dismissal to finish; never let a keyboard-covered
coordinate become a financial action. Inspect expanded forms and retained tab stacks before continuing,

the exact known synthetic PDF under the existing native-grant/envelope/size gates, not arbitrary files.
Use a fresh fictitious profile, retire only that profile through original confirmations, verify prior
public profiles/Home/OS/APK and remove only owned hash-matched inputs/receiver before releasing the turn.


## D-382 withdrawn required GUID storage markers

Do not repeat the two-required-GUID storage-descriptor candidate without new measured evidence.
Fewer per-row native calls did not improve the complete original Home cohort. Keep the D-377 reader
and retain required identity TEXT/BLOB/legacy/error behavior against an independent EF oracle. Preserve
all original fields, conversion/refusal order, query/interceptor/transaction and actual-file authority;
do not promote a micro-level rationale, select fast samples or extend a rejected candidate to the phone.

## D-383 bounded recent Home selection

Home's captured complete ledger is already stable Date/CreatedAt descending. Select the first three
nonfuture original rows on its existing facts worker; never rescan/sort older history on the window
thread or reduce financial calculation inputs. Preserve references, stable ties and original presenter.
Independent original-order/packet oracles run outside measured clocks/heartbeat and do not alter old
financial baseline files. Retain native crashes and completed cases; bounded public notification
intervals do not establish whole-Home speed, general runtime stability or release acceptance.

## D-384 unaccepted combined GC/nursery configuration

The isolated serial-major/64MiB-nursery trial is not a production fix: complete finite native/data
cohorts pass but observed dispatcher pauses worsen and memory/long-term stability remain unqualified.
Do not repeat it unchanged, extend it to the phone or promote it from a clean sample/upstream report.
Keep ordinary Main/runtime policy, original full workloads and actual negative evidence. Separate
post-terminal aggregate memory from peak, leak, low-memory and comparative claims; no raw memory dumps.


## D-385 - Complete bounded CSV input

Selected CSV is a complete transient review, never a truncated financial stream. Count actual bytes
rather than provider Length; retain BOM-aware decoding across fragmented reads and bound decoded
characters, fields, rows, columns and cells independently of commercial quotas. Refuse unfinished
quotes and empty/header-only replacements before retiring the previous review or accepted outcome.
Keep stream opening/decoding/parsing and original matching work off the window thread; publish only
a complete prepared result for the still-current profile and interpretation, including final awaits.
Never archive or log raw selected CSV. Preserve stricter bank limits and explicit consent policies.
Qualify only changed behavior; D-386's later stability/accessibility execution order remains in force.


## D-387 - Preserve CSV financial meaning at quote boundaries

Never repair malformed CSV by stripping quotation marks or joining quoted and unquoted amount digits.
A closing field quote must meet the delimiter, record end or EOF; quotes inside a nonquoted field
are refused. Preserve escaped literal quotes, Unicode, multiline values, existing delimiter/record
choices and unquoted whitespace. Refuse the whole replacement through the existing retained-review
path with accurate six-language feedback. This is source integrity, not a new bank-format claim.

## D-390 unaccepted supported new bridge candidate

The isolated concurrent-major/new-bridge cohort preserves complete original native/data behavior but
has longer dispatcher gaps than every historical corresponding sample. Do not promote it, extend it
to the phone or repeat it unchanged. Keep ordinary Main/runtime policy and original negative evidence;
finite correctness is not general crash/ANR acceptance. Restore ordinary caller/Main/OS state and
distinguish terminal aggregate memory from peak/leak/low-memory claims. No raw memory collection.

## D-391 bounded original reader speech evidence

Actual reader speech-fragment text/locale/role can qualify a request boundary, not audible pronunciation
or all-reader acceptance. Keep gestures, actual native focus and original context/timestamps together;
utterance IDs can restart on reconnection. Any temporary reader diagnostic preference is limited to
the authorized fictitious emulator, with exact native/secure setting restoration and no archived
general logs, accounts, secrets or private-file extraction. Empty overlay observations are not silence.
Do not repeat unchanged request captures or old waveform/ASR attempts to manufacture audible acceptance.


## D-392 - Immutable import Undo and retained aggregate fields

Every new ledger import, including ordinary and explicit Keep both batches, records complete immutable
original rows through the existing journal in the same write transaction. Never reconstruct an old
review from current rows: missing legacy snapshots, later edits/attachments, extra batch rows and
dependent imports/refunds require review without deleting data. Retain original metadata through
backup/restore. Existing aggregates expose their complete original range in Simple mode after all
loaded values are published; an unsaved off/on change must not hide the switch. Qualify actual
financial failures using the same fixture and immutable input, repeating only failed changed paths.

## D-393 - Original physical bulk command qualification

Bulk financial qualification uses a fresh fictitious profile, exact original selections and explicit
native actions. Preserve selection across filters, cancellation and reload; never substitute a new
financial action for a failed observer. Observe Delete and invoke original Undo within its unchanged
deadline, without navigation, clock replacement or renewed offers. Planned cold persistence follows
terminal Undo only. Retain complete original metadata/relationships and restore Main/profiles/OS/APK,
remove only owned matched inputs and release the coordinated phone turn before ending the batch.

## D-394 - Refresh retained account tabs without financial replay

Account detail activation after another Shell tab must use its original covered/coalesced loader,
preserving unsaved reconciliation amount/reason/date and removing retired-shell subscriptions.
Distinguish correctly persisted money from stale retained presentation. Repeat only the failed
changed navigation path, never an accepted save to repair an observer. Native review preserves
completed contexts, actual digit conversion and the same partially edited fixture. Architecture
source exceptions stay exact, build/package bounded and negative-tested; never exempt Diagnostics.

## D-395 - Plans Add follows Home's title action

Home and Plans keep the original real Add command in their own growing title row, physically right,
with a48px visual/target and complete translated name. Do not reserve a bottom action band or add a
second Shell/Windows title. Preserve original Back for a pushed page, plan filters/warnings and the
separate empty-state action. Qualify only the changed page; no financial Save is needed for layout.

## D-396 - Original balance comparison and atomic correction

Amount/date/account changes retire the original reconciliation review without discarding typed
drafts. Confirm only the same account/file/date/observed amount/reason; changed history/access or
pending drafts require a fresh comparison. Keep adjustment and current stored account reconciliation
metadata in the same existing financial writer. Never overwrite a stale form account, regress a
later reconciled date or replay a committed write after notification failure. Independent native
qualification preserves accepted financial cases; repair only failed observation on the same fixture.

## D-397 - Retained final advance-bill outcome

The original settlement form owns one accepted financial outcome. Mark acceptance before native
return and preserve it through nonfatal subscriber/navigation failures. Original Save and Cancel
become Back-only after commit; never reload, retarget or repost that form as another bill. Keep
pre-write drafts and actual-file/currency/retired-input guards. Coherent reads publish before input
is enabled. Native observer failures preserve accepted money; continue only the failed observation
or not-yet-executed contexts, with explicit retained-case readback rather than another Save.

## D-398 - Same-file Home supersession under the original cover

Only persisted source changes with unchanged original Home/plan/goal packets and current actual-file
authority permit bounded full rereads. Validate all retained inputs before classifying a refusal
and again on its typed boundary; never catch arbitrary InvalidOperationException as a retry signal.
Keep the original cover and file across at most three attempts, then explicit Retry. Normal reviewed
writes, choices and template handoffs stay strict. Preserve completed native contexts; qualify new
reference-addition paths without replaying accepted money or unchanged successful matrices.

## D-399 - Original occurrence command outcomes after commit

Foreground occurrence commands preserve known acceptance across nonfatal ledger/plan notification
failures. Retire the original form before fallible refresh/navigation/feedback and hold the gate
through its native alert. Reopening retains the exact original file-bound deletion result; ordinary
ledger deletion and automatic posting keep their existing contracts. Do not turn pre-commit errors
or cancelled consent into acceptance. Native dialogs may have title-named windows: require the
original focused activity plus exact owned message/button, preserving one input intent per answer.
Observer/encoding repairs continue the same process and accepted fixtures without financial replay.

## D-400 - Newer accepted occurrence state owns the correction boundary

Before any foreground link SQL or occurrence-state mutation, recheck the actual state in the same
writer. A retained open/skipped form cannot replace a newer settlement; pending link consent cannot
replace a newer skip. Preserve same-entry link idempotency and the separate explicit reopen path.
Native refusal diagnostics retain accepted rows and drafts. Classify only the exact four deliberate
failure records with complete original receipts; unexpected failures remain rejected. Do not replay
accepted financial actions to repair a diagnostic observer.

## D-401 - Retained occurrence reads preserve the original draft

Actual retained Shell-tab activation refreshes the original occurrence's candidates under one shared
read cover. Do not reseed raw amount/date/note inputs during that refresh, even temporarily. A
changed financial target refuses publication until the form is reviewed again. Failed reads retain
the covered draft with translated Retry; do not expose stale writable fields. A pending original
financial command retains its busy gate and consent, while its accepted canonical reload can still
reset defaults. Keep the physical stale-candidate evidence and accepted manual row; qualify a
changed source caption without posting that money again, then finish only remaining lifecycle work.

## D-403 - Original portable restore across an independent fresh install

Fresh-install qualification must actually uninstall/reinstall only a fixed, freshly owned independent
complete signed Release package; an empty new profile is not equivalent. Preserve permanent Main,
its profiles, OS values and credentials. Export only fictitious data through the original explicit
native grant, retain its exact matched public file, then use original first-run preview/consent/restore.
Compare the original re-export's whole schema/typed rows and portable settings, distinguishing original
onboarding's Settings.UpdatedAt ticks from financial changes. Never extract installed private stores
or keys, replay accepted writes for an observer, reuse a different project's package, or turn this
same-phone unprotected test into protected-password/new-device/iOS/release acceptance.

## D-404 - Original card payment and archive qualification

Positive credit after a card payment remains a transfer outcome, not new income or another expense.
Archive qualification reviews both source/destination plan relationships and dirty draft discard, then
preserves stored financial identity/history and clears only the matching default. Unarchive must not
resume ended plans or guess a default. Preserve accepted money and continue only pending observations;
restore Main and retire only the newly owned inactive fixture before releasing a coordinated phone turn.

## D-405 - Ordinary CSV original financial review

Own/mapped CSV must carry its coherent original file/account/category/history/journal packet into the
same existing atomic writer. Bind exact proposed entries; reject changed facts before money, keep raw
input/choices and require explicit Refresh. Ordinary read-only review uses fresh writer authorization;
it does not become an authorization bypass or a new purchase gate. Preserve bank reviewed consent,
holdings and accepted-result contracts. Retire departed forms, and never replay accepted import/Undo
to repair a native observer. Independent continuation checkpoints retain original data and evidence.

## D-406 - Retained original import tab departure

Android routed More pages may leave their visible tab without OnDisappearing. Retire the original
CSV financial review on uncancelled Shell tab navigation after existing leave guards, retaining raw
source and choices for explicit Refresh. Never replay accepted import/Undo or opening-date edits to
repair observers; use final-candidate read-only preview/departure and preserve the original cold data.


## D-407 - Original split/join review and accepted outcome

Read split/group, account, category/refund and attachment metadata coherently from the actual file.
Validate the immutable source and exact pending shares under the existing financial writer before
EntryActions mutates copies. Preserve rights, total and attachment ownership. A stale source keeps
raw parts but retires authority; known commit retires the original form before fallible publication
or return. Preserve successful refusal/acceptance receipts and original fixtures; observer repair
continues only pending work without reseeding or replaying accepted money. Modal focus belongs to
the original owned native modal, not an Activity behind it. Restore Main/profiles/OS and retire only
the newly owned inactive profile before releasing a coordinated phone turn.


## D-408 - Original holding editor review

Keep original event/type/history/location/account/group/price facts coherent and revalidate them in
the existing quantity/payment/fee/derived-price writer. Mutate copies, preserve original rights and
reject foreign money/event identities. Adopt one explicit new location only if every other original
fact stays unchanged. Empty native query redelivery must retain the already loaded review. Known
commit retires the original form before fallible notification/return. Preserve accepted cases and
continue only the exact uncompleted native step; no reseeding or replay to repair an observer.


## D-409 - Refund boundaries after source corrections

Validate the final retained refund amounts and receiving currencies against each corrected expense
source inside the existing ordinary/holding atomic writer. Preserve valid explicit fee deletion/unlink
and complete final-batch correction; do not convert, unlink or relabel money to make an invalid edit
pass. Keep field-local account/fee errors, raw drafts and the original review available for correction.
After original native acceptance, caption checks may rehydrate only retained rejected presentation
with retired authority; never replay financial commands to repair an observer. This does not validate
malformed optional fee text or accept wider physical/platform/runtime gates.


## D-410 - Optional holding fee input is not a zero fallback

Only blank or a successfully parsed zero requests removal of an optional holding fee. Nonblank
unparseable, negative or out-of-range text refuses before financial mutation and retains the exact
draft/original review for explicit correction. Hidden nonfinancial fee drafts create no money.
Preview must not guess a zero fee or wrap account changes. Native feedback checks compare the
existing native digit conversion with source text; keep original source/draft evidence. Continuation
after a terminal observer failure uses exact original accepted files read-only and never replays money.

## D-411 - Refundable holding fees retain their own action

An eligible fee expense keeps its existing Record refund action even while source edit/delete and
other managed holding mutations stay under the original holding history. Do not expose principal as
income/spending or guess refund links. Preserve the original purchase on a UI failure; continue only
pending refund/correction/sale and one planned cold read. Native semantics use Android accessibility
nodes when MAUI supplies a delegate; a direct View property mismatch is not missing user text. Keep
failed independent modal/route observations separate from actual device acceptance and never replay
accepted financial operations or seeds to repair a diagnostic observer. Restore Main/public packets/
profiles/OS and retire only the new inactive fixture before releasing a coordinated phone turn.

## D-412 - Original quantity range refusal

Check parsed quantities against the stored base-unit range before multiplying. Keep exact representable

## D-413 - Original manual price review and accepted outcome

Manual holding-price Save and confirmed delete revalidate the original coherent type/history/price/

retained Android tab departures retire authority. Preserve accepted native receipts/files. A failed

replaying its seed; distinguish a later metadata control from the original quantity-change case.

## D-414 - Original holding history deletion and short Undo

Review the original displayed history row/group and explicit delete confirmation coherently, then

immutable, single-use deletion receipt with explicit refund links and orphan attachment ownership/
content. Reject changed/foreign/mutated/consumed Undo before money; recheck the restored packet and

Known delete/Undo acceptance survives fallible notification/readback without resubmission. Retained

replays no accepted command or seed. Keep the existing short-offer/startup orphan policy; no durable

## D-415 - Archive only the original reviewed holding

Archive confirmation revalidates the original coherent displayed actual-file type/history/prices/

tracked entity; never resubmit a retained whole-type object. Consume known acceptance before fallible
notification/return and preserve the separate raw-price draft. Missing/failed Refresh cannot rearm
stale or accepted authority. Native continuation uses exact completed receipts/files read-only and
never replays an accepted archive or seed. Keep D-386 priorities and separate type-editor/device gates.


## D-416 - Original type form identity and known acceptance

Type creation owns one stable absent identity; edit owns the original actual-file type/history/price
packet. Copy before mutation and recheck fresh rights, original source and pending raw input under
the writer. Keep noneditable metadata and existing measurement rules. Empty query redelivery retains
the original draft/identity, including a missing-row refusal; never reinterpret it as creation.
Retire stale/departed authority without replacing raw input. Known Save consumes the form before
notification/navigation and leaves a failed following step closed to resubmission. Read-only native
captions use exact completed receipts/files and original raw values, never accepted seeds/commands.

## D-417 - Original physical holding lifecycle evidence

Physical qualification preserves one original form/file/financial sequence, with each accepted write
invoked once. Continue failed host observations only on retained originals; never replay accepted
money to repair a caption check. A value packet can include its source/date suffix. A category chip
tap alone is not proof of its final selected state; report the observed cold category honestly.
Short holding Undo is exercised immediately in its original view/process, before the one terminal
cold. Restore original Main/public packets/profile set/OS and retire only the new inactive fixture
before releasing the coordinated phone turn. Retain D-386 priorities and wider platform gates.

## D-418 - Original coherent holding overview

Read existing overview settings/types/events/prices/places through one actual-file worker transaction,
without initializing settings. Prepare the whole packet before covered publication; retry only bounded
same-file supersession. Refuse changed file/input/rights, retire departed work and recheck exact current
row/file before native navigation even under inactive test policy. Preserve the explicit local Retry.
Native semantic checks compare the actual bound description, not blanket digit conversion of user
identifiers. Continue a terminal observer failure only on exact original files/checkpoints and retain
original Case PIDs; caption-only correction never replays accepted navigation, seeds or financial work.
Keep D-386 priorities and wider physical/platform/release gates separate.

## D-419 - Original complete detail and routed type ownership

Read original detail facts and existing settings through one captured actual-file worker transaction;
never initialize settings for display. Prepare all fields before covered publication and revalidate
source/file/rights. Keep explicit Retry, missing state and same-type raw price Refresh distinct.
Theme recoloring never rereads or retires raw financial input. New routed identity owns new editors;
old draft/accepted outcome/short Undo and retained native targets cannot transfer to it. Gate each
editor by its original identity/instance. Bind refresh/readback callbacks to their captured lifetime,
including a known commit completed after departure; they cannot reactivate that view. Retire full detail work on ordinary or retained-tab
departure. Preserve completed typed original fixtures and accepted native contexts; only changed
query controls continue, without seed or financial replay. D-386 and wider external gates stand.

## D-420 - Original holding-place creation

Own the original name prompt before Save can run. Create only one stable absent place identity in
the original actual-file writer with unchanged source, fresh rights and pending owner checked before
commit. Preserve raw inputs/name/id across uncommitted retry. Keep a known metadata result separate
from financial acceptance; postcommit/readback/native publication failure offers read-only choice
refresh, never another create. Refuse renamed/foreign/stale/departed adoption. Transfer retains its
source and adopts only the destination. A new explicit query waits for its retired metadata writer.
Retained native captions copy original acceptance with all writer/recovery authority retired; no
accepted metadata/financial seed or command is replayed for observer correction. D-386 still applies.

## D-421 - Complete original holding-event form

Cover original event reads and complete field publication, with original-file Retry/Cancel after
failure. Do not initialize settings or create default metadata before validating the original event/
type. Required missing-default scaffolding retains Corrections permission; explicit extra places
still require ManageHoldings. Retain known default identity through readback/publication failure,
refuse its later removal and never recreate it on Retry. Published raw-draft Retry is read-only;
unavailable original kind/account/place is not a guessed replacement. Preserve historical archived
payment/place identity and rebind transfer choices by id. A new routed identity owns fresh raw input
and a separate session; an old accepted callback cannot close or rearm it. Preserve native original
files/rows/PIDs, with no accepted seed/financial replay. D-386 and external gates remain separate.

## D-423 - Native popup observation boundaries

An early active-window-only hierarchy or empty raw Android ContentDescription is not proof that a
popup has no visible actions. Observe the original owned native panel after realization, read its
actual accessibility-node name and verify visible target geometry before one native invocation.
Retain negative observations and unchanged stored rows; never ship a speculative viewport workaround
or replay an accepted financial sequence to repair an observer. Keep physical currency input and
spoken accessibility gates distinct from this independent no-Save selection check.

## D-424 - Original detail attachment work

Keep a native document pick/confirmation bound to its original displayed entry, exact file and view
revision. Refuse another configured file before entry/attachment queries and recheck original facts,
rights, bytes and pending owner under the writer. Hold no database context across native awaits.
Consume known metadata commits before fallible notification/publication; never repeat a pick or
remove after accepted work. Give file identity its own complete row and separate real action targets.
Preserve completed native cases and original fixtures; a native exit after a complete report remains
a recorded failure, not general stability acceptance. Keep D-386 priorities and external gates.

## D-425 - Original editor acceptance and receipt destination

Bind an editor financial Save to its original actual file, cached rights, raw-draft revision and view
owner. Detach the submitted batch before awaits. Capture its exact saved entry/document destination
inside the financial writer and consume acceptance immediately after commit, before fallible change
notification. A later attachment failure never rearms or reclassifies saved money; another profile,
changed saved source or departed owner cannot receive it. Retire pending callbacks on departure/new
query while retaining raw input and known acceptance. Native qualification follows routed construction:
create the real page before publishing its query. Continue failed observations on the original
uncommitted fixture; never repeat an accepted seed or Save to repair an observer. D-386 still applies.

## D-426 - Complete original transaction details

Read original detail/reference/group/refund/holding/document/rule facts in one actual-file worker
transaction; do not initialize settings for display. Cover full publication and expose explicit
read-only Retry/missing states. A routed identity owns fresh command instances; old confirmations,
template prompts, receipt/viewer work and departed callbacks cannot follow another publication.
Retain an unsaved template name only for its original file and entry.
Review changes only the original flag. Rules use original category/match; equivalent existing rules
are not written again. Delete retains exact original file/group/refund/document Undo and its deadline.
Consume known commits before notification; following recovery only rereads. A refused viewer removes
only its newly created never-dispatched cache file. Preserve original source/PID receipts after report
failure; no seed or accepted command replay. D-386 priorities and external acceptance gates stand.

## D-427 - Original transaction editor publication and raw ownership

Prepare the complete original editor packet in one actual-file worker transaction without creating
settings. Cover every field and derived currency/fee flag before enabling commands. Explicit missing
sources never become blank new entries. A fresh routed identity clears all optional raw fields,
including the icon; retain historical account references and require an explicit/default new account
rather than guessing. Bind detail/template/receipt handoffs, commands, focus and confirmations to
their original owner. Read-only refresh retains raw input and waits for its exact pending writer.
Validate the original group and sibling refunds under the writer; preserve both duplicate fee values
with fresh identities. Consume known acceptance before notification and never rearm it.
Preserve original native action checkpoints before fallible observers. Reconstruct only a failed
caption from its exact source, with explicit prior-action evidence and limits; never replay an
ambiguous or accepted confirmation/Save. Native visible text extents exclude trailing whitespace.
D-428 aggregate ownership remains separate; retain D-386 and external gates.

## D-428 - Original nested account and aggregate workflow

An explicit nested account visit retains its original parent raw/receipt draft and actual-file
rights. Adopt only its known committed reference packet; retire parent/child callbacks on departure
and wait for their exact writer. Commit new account/default atomically. Known notification failure
offers Close without another create. First-account receipt selection never overwrites typed money.
Review each newly introduced aggregate coverage edge separately. Revalidate the complete detached
proposal, choices, coverage and document/refund/prior-consumption facts under the original writer.
Never subtract an unchanged edge or consume one detail twice. Keep manual source identities and use
the existing profile-local adjustment journal. Short original-file Undo restores aggregates, retains
details and refuses expiry, dependencies or changed rows; generic import Undo cannot bypass it.
Consume financial/Undo acceptance before notification. Full monetary review belongs in a native
dialog body with short actions. Home/Transactions show the actual adjustment meaning only for the
original remaining deadline. Preserve original fixtures/accepted actions; no observer repair replay.
Retain D-386 priorities and external gates.

## D-429 - Complete original plan and debt drafts

Publish the original account/category/settings/plan/state/payment facts from one actual-file worker
read transaction without initializing settings. Missing explicit sources remain unavailable. A fresh
query owns fresh raw fields and commands; ordinary refresh retains raw input. Readiness failure
keeps complete fields editable while Save is disabled; explicit same-input review may renew cached
rights, never silently replace the original source. Original transaction-to-plan handoffs retire
with their parent. Nested account return adopts only its known accepted packet; debt payment needs
an active money account in the original currency, without guessing conversion or principal.
Revalidate original facts under the financial writer, including Open overrides as history. A split
retains its original recurrence anchor and moves only exact future payment/state owners, never
recorded money. Consume acceptance before notification; failed observers leave a clean Close-only
saved form. Retire reminder/confirmation callbacks with the original frame. Preserve accepted native
actions and source checkpoints; continue failed observations without another account/plan Save.
D-386 runtime/accessibility order and external acceptance gates remain unchanged.

## D-430 - Original monetary meaning in plan drafts

Capture each raw source/destination amount's immutable parse culture, ISO currency and explicit
display unit. Display changes never reinterpret typed money. Keep its original label/example;
currency/unit changes require clearing and actual reentry, never a partial edit or automatic
conversion. Hidden/unknown inputs neither post stale amounts nor show irrelevant format feedback.
Retain contexts through read-only refresh and nested return. Original writer guards include input
identity and monetary edit revision; identical text after another edit cannot revive pending work.
Preserve known acceptance and original native checkpoints. Observe fully realized account choices
before introducing a fictitious draft, and continue only incomplete observations. Actual focused
native views, not a stale Activity reference, establish independent rendering ownership. Retain
D-386 priorities and all physical/provider/Mac/store/publication gates.

## D-431 - Original goal and budget monetary inputs

Capture target/contribution and total/category units independently. Display changes never reinterpret
raw money. Currency/unit changes require complete clearing/reentry; partial edits cannot renew meaning.
Retain dirty contexts through reload, including identical digits in a new unit. Expose stale hidden
limit review; ignore irrelevant share amounts and preserve physical quantity rules. Check raw revisions
and native ownership under Save/cut writers. Consume commits before notification and never rearm them.
Native review awaits the exact deferred tab rebuild before opening another frame. Normalize only
selected digit shapes when comparing native labels; retain punctuation and source text. Continue
incomplete observations from exact checkpoints without accepted-action replay. D-386 and external
gates remain unchanged.

## D-432 - Original transaction and split monetary inputs

Retain primary/destination/fee/ISO-purchase/reimbursable contexts independently. Display changes never
reinterpret raw money; changed currencies/units require complete clearing/reentry. Preserve original
labels/examples, account Undo and nested/read-only return. Explicit receipt/template adoption uses
authoritative minor units. Plan handoff ignores unsupported fee details. Split shares keep original
meaning; Join uses the reviewed stored total. Validate raw/context revision under original writers.
Known acceptance never rearms. Native continuation requires exact source checkpoints; an accepted
receipt without its final observer checkpoint refuses another Save. Await the original parent's full
return publication before reading readiness. D-386 priorities and external gates stand.

## D-433 - Preserve CI policy boundaries during fixture/toolchain recovery

Keep mode-policy fixture exceptions explicit and verify each complete outer review-only build gate;
never exempt an entire source directory or ordinary Release reader. Shared-membership Undo tests use
an actual original committed deletion receipt before revoking membership; fabricated groups exercise
a different provenance refusal. Installer inventory changes never approve compiler bytes. Retain exact
compiler hash/file-version/signature, MSVC/SDK and immutable package requirements. Local metadata
checks do not establish actual hosted success. App/device checks are not replayed for test-only changes;
CI recovery requires the latest commit's actual workflow evidence. D-386 and external gates stand.


### D-433 original Linux host restoration

A pinned base does not freeze a moving archive. Restore the original signed Ubuntu snapshot and final
locked host packages. Bootstrap HTTPS trust in a separate exact-release stage; copy only its public
bundle, never moving dependencies. Require complete signed indexes, retain TLS/signature checks and
every NDK/compiler/source/package gate. Local restoration is not hosted native or product acceptance.

## D-434 - Original account money and default follow-up

Keep opening, installment and observed-balance input contexts independent. Preserve raw currency/unit/
culture and original unit-factor notes; require complete clearing/reentry to adopt a changed unit.
Unknown principal hides only its own input. Preserve historical debt signs and decimal-rate culture.
Bind pending work to original file/presentation and monetary edit revision. Check stored currency
before match metadata or adjustment authority. Default selection after known account acceptance may
touch only original existing settings under metadata ownership; switched files/departed owners refuse
